Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on precipitation structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 807. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 981 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to precipitation structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 807/981 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
